mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub></math>
 </p><p>As clear by above electronic configurations that, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> has two unpaired electrons and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo></msup></math>  Each has only one unpaired electron; hence magnetic moment of  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></msup></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>73</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q46. JEE Main 2020 (08 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Arrange the following bonds according to their average bond energies in descending order:
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>F</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>l</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>r</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>r</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>l</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>F</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>r</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>l</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>F</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>l</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>r</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>F</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>F</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>l</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>r</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Bond energy <math><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>d</mi><mtext>  </mtext><mi>l</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>t</mi><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

Bond length order in carbon halogen bonds arein the order of C – F < C – Cl < C – Br < C – IHence, Bond energy orderC – F > C – Cl > C – Br > C – I

</details>

### Q47. JEE Main 2020 (07 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The bond order and the magnetic characteristic of <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> diamagnetic
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> diamagnetic
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> paramagnetic
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> paramagnetic

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> diamagnetic**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>According to MOT (If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> is internuclear axis) The configuration of

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mtext>CN</mtext><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup><mo>:</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup></math>
 
Bond order<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>number</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>electrons</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>in</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>bonding</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>orbitals</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>number</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>electrons</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>in</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>anti</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>bonding</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>orbitals</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math> <mrow> <mtext>Bond&#160;order&#160;</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>CN</mtext></mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </math> is diamagnetic due to absence of unpaired electron.</p>

</details>

### Q48. JEE Main 2019 (10 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

During the change of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> to <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> , the incoming electron goes to the orbital:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">σ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Molecular orbital configuration of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> ( <math><mn>16</mn></math> electrons) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>
When an electron is added, it enters the <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub></math> orbital and the configuration becomes <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi ### Q49. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Among the following species, the diamagnetic molecule is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

(1) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></mover><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup></math> , paramagnetic
(2) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup></math> , paramagnetic
(3) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo></math> paramagnetic
(4) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>,</mo></math> diamagnetic (No unpaired electron)

</details>

### Q50. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

Among the following, the molecule expected to be stabilized by anion formation is:
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>According to molecular orbital theory the configuration is like that
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2.5</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> bond order increases so it is more favourable.
For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msub></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msubsup></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.5</mn></math>
Bond order decreases <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> so not favourable.
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.5</mn></math>
Bond order decreases <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> so not favourable
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>1</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>0</mn></msubsup></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2.5</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
BO <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> decreases so not favourable</p><p>
In case of only <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo></math> incoming electron will enter the boding molecular orbital which increases the bond order and stability.</p><p>Whereas rest of all takes incoming electron in their antibonding molecular orbital which decreased stability.</p>

</details>

### Q52. JEE Main 2019 (10 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

Two pi and half sigma bonds are present in:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msubsup><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msubsup><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math > <mrow>  <msubsup>   <mi>O</mi>   <mn>2</mn>   <mo>&#x2212;</mo>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C3;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>1</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C3;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>2</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>x</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C0;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>y</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C0;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>z</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C0;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>y</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C0;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>z</mi>   <mn>1</mn>  </msubsup>  </mrow></math>

Bond Order <math > <mrow>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>6</mn><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mn>1.5</mn></mrow></math>

<math > <mrow>  <msub>   <mi>O</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msub>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C3;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>1</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C3;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>2</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>x</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C0;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>y</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C0;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>z</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C0;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>y</mi>   <mn>1</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C0;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>z</mi>   <mn>1</mn>  </msubsup>  </mrow></math>

Bond order <math > <mrow>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>6</mn><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></math>

<math > <mrow>  <msub>   <mi>N</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msub>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C3;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>1</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C3;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>2</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C0;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>z</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C0;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>z</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>x</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  </mrow></math>

Bond order <math > <mrow>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>6</mn><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></math>

According to <math><mi>M</mi><mi>O</mi><mi>T</mi></math> ; electronic configuration of <math><msubsup><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> is:

<math > <mrow>  <msubsup>   <mi>N</mi>   <mn>2</mn>   <mo>+</mo>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C3;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>1</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mover>   <mi>&#x03C3;</mi>   <mo>*</mo>  </mover>  <mn>2</mn><msup>   <mi>s</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C0;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>x</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>=</mo><mi>&#x03C0;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>y</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msubsup>  <mo>&#x003C;</mo><mi>&#x03C3;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup>   <mi>p</mi>   <mi>z</mi>   <mn>1</mn>  </msubsup>  </mrow></math>

Bond order <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2.5</mn></math>

i.e., <math><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></math> bonds and <math><mn>0.5</mn><mtext> </mtext><mi>σ</mi></math> bonds.

</details>

### Q53. JEE Main 2019 (09 Jan Shift 2)

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.5</mn></math>

Having 1- unpaired <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> so paramagnetic bond order decrease

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math><msub><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><mi>p</mi><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

No unpaired <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> so diamagnetic

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>&#963;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>*</mi></mrow></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2.5</mn></math>

Having one unpaired <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> so paramagnetic

Bond order increases <math><msub><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> to <math><msubsup><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>

</details>

### Q54. JEE Main 2019 (09 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

According to molecular orbital theory, which of the following is true with respect to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi>Li</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></msubsup></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi>Li</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></msubsup></math> ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mi> </mi></math> is stable and <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mi> </mi></math> is unstable
- **(B)** Both are unstable
- **(C)** Both are stable
- **(D)** <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mi> </mi></math> is unstable and <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mi> </mi></math> is stable

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) Both are stable**

**Detailed Solution:**

According to molecular orbital theory  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>  has 5 electrons  <math><mo>-</mo><mi>σ</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>σ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>σ</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>  has 7 electrons  <math><mo>-</mo><mi>σ</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mi>σ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi>σ</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mi>σ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>*</mi></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mtext>  </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mtext>  </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> 

Bond order of  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> 

Bond order of  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> 

As both have similar bond order, both  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>  and  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>  will be stable.

Note:  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>  will be relatively unstable to  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>  as it has more electrons in anti bonding orbitals, so have more energy and having more repulsion so  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>  is less stable as  <math><mi>L</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>

</details>

### Q55. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 2 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

Which of the following best describes the diagram of molecular orbital?
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/mvtXkZ6kZPjy49KKhDKhiP86mp3Q2mKs62btivEvdtI.original.fullsize.png">

**Options:**

- **(A)** A bonding $\pi$ orbital
- **(B)** A non-bonding orbital
- **(C)** An antibonding $\sigma$ orbital
- **(D)** An antibonding $\pi$ orbital

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) An antibonding $\pi$ orbital**

**Detailed Solution:**

An antibonding $\pi$ orbital best describes the diagram of a molecular orbital. Two orbitals laterally overlap to form pi bond. Out of phase combination, of these two $p$ orbitals, give $\pi^*$ MO.

</details>

### Q56. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

<p> <img height="47" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/2aea37bb-86c1-4332-b2c5-97940a265198-image.png" width="146" />
In the above compound, the bond orders of bonds <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced></math> are:</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Azide ion <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo></msubsup></mfenced></math>    

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/decdc674-c506-4d0f-b5c4-d6ff7fb6c365-image.png" style="width: 300px; height: 160px;" />

### Q57. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

In the molecular orbital diagram for the molecular ion <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></msubsup></math> , the number of electrons in the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> molecular orbital is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></msubsup><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>]</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>

The number of electron in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msub></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>.

</details>

### Q58. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 1 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/T-881E9A1vfmyRL0aWS31lq4kjp3XTCXlxC_gKro28Q.original.fullsize.png">

In hydrogen azide, the bond orders of bonds (I) and (II) are ______.

**Options:**

- **(A)** I $ < 2$, II $>2$
- **(B)** I $>2$, II $>2$
- **(C)** I$>2$, II $ < 2$
- **(D)** I $ < 2$, II $ < 2$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) I $ < 2$, II $>2$**

**Detailed Solution:**

As in the resonance structure of hydrogen azide, it can be seen that number of $\mathrm{N}-\mathrm{N}$ bonds is $\leq 2$
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/ulI3xDDfrs-jMCBQqsNyj588Er65_mZiXDAWqNbYwug.original.fullsize.png">

Hence for bond (I), bond order will be $ < 2$ whereas for bond (II), number of bond $\geq 2$. 
Thus its bond order will be $>2$.

</details>

### Q59. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 1 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

In the molecular orbital diagram for the molecular ion, $\mathrm{N}_2^{+}$, the number of electrons in the $\sigma_{2 p}$ molecular orbital is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** $0$
- **(B)** $2$
- **(C)** $3$
- **(D)** $1$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $1$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Total electrons in $\mathrm{N}_2^{+}=(7 \times 2)-1=13$
$\mathrm{N}_2^{+} \rightarrow \sigma_{1 s^2}, \sigma_{1 s^2}^*, \sigma_{2 s^2}, \sigma_{2 s^2}^*,\left[\pi_{2p_x}^2=\pi_{2 p_y}^2\right] \sigma_{2 p_z}^1$
Number of electron in $\sigma_{2 p_z}$ is $1$.

</details>

### Q60. JEE Main 2018 (08 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

According to molecular orbital theory, which of the following molecule will not be available?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi>He</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi>He</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></msubsup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></msubsup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The electronic configuration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8854;</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">*</mi></msup><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/266689.jpg" style="width: 256px; height: 177px;" />

Bond order of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8861;</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></msub></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8854;</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> does not exist, due to zero bond order.

</details>

### Q61. JEE Main 2017 (08 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Which of the following is paramagnetic?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>CO</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>NO</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Number of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>CO</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>NO</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>.

According to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>MOT</mi></math>,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>CO</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

no unpaired e<sup>-</sup> so diamagnetic

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>B</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>:</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup> <mi>s</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>&#60;</mo><mover> <mi>&#963;</mi> <mo>*</mo> </mover> <mn>1</mn><msup> <mi>s</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup> <mi>s</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>&#60;</mo><mover> <mi>&#963;</mi> <mo>*</mo> </mover> <mn>2</mn><msup> <mi>s</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub> <mi>p</mi> <mi>x</mi> </msub> <msup> <mrow></mrow> <mn>1</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msub> <mi>p</mi> <mi>y</mi> </msub> <msup> <mrow></mrow> <mn>1</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math> unpaired <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math>,so paramagnetic

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>:</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

no unpaired <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math>, so diamagnetic

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>NO</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>:</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></mover><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn><msup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

no unpaired <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math>, so diamagnetic.

</details>

### Q62. JEE Main 2017 (02 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Which of the following species is not paramagnetic?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mtext>C</mtext><mtext>O</mtext></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>B</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mtext>N</mtext><mtext>O</mtext></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mtext>C</mtext><mtext>O</mtext></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

CO has no unpaired electron while  other have unpaired electrons so, paramagnetic in nature.(Apply M.O.T)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>Molecular&#160;orbital&#160;configuration</mtext><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>CO&#160;</mtext><mo>:</mo><msub><mtext>like&#160;N</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mtext>&#160;</mtext><mo>;</mo><mtext>&#160;&#963;</mtext><mn>1</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><msup><mtext>1S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mi>&#963;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>x</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext>=&#160;&#960;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>y</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>z</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#160;</mtext><mo>(</mo><mtext>No&#160;unpaired&#160;electrons)</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>O</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mtext>&#160;</mtext><mo>:</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>1</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><msup><mtext>1S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>z</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>x</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>y</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>x</mtext><mtext>1</mtext></msubsup><mtext>=&#160;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>y</mtext><mtext>1</mtext></msubsup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#160;</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext>unpaired&#160;electrons</mtext><mo>)</mo></mrow></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>B</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>1</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>x</mtext><mn>1</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>y</mtext><mtext>1</mtext></msubsup><mtext>&#160;</mtext><mo>(2</mo><mtext>unpaired&#160;electrons</mtext><mo>)</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>NO&#160;</mtext><mo>:</mo><mtext>&#160;</mtext><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>1</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>1</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>S</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#963;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>z</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>x</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>y</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#60;</mo><msup><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>y</mtext><mtext>1</mtext></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msup><mtext>&#160;&#960;</mtext><mo>*</mo></msup><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>x</mtext><mn>0</mn></msubsup><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext>unpaired&#160;electrons</mtext><mo>)</mo></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q63. JEE Main 2015 (10 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

After understanding the assertion and reason, choose the correct option.

<strong>Assertion: </strong>In the bonding molecular orbital (MO) of <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow><mo>,</mo> </math> electron density is increased between the nuclei.
<strong>Reason:</strong> The bonding MO is <math><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">&#968;</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">A</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">&#968;</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">B</mtext></mrow></msub></math> , which shows destructive interference of the combining electron waves.

**Options:**

- **(A)** Assertion and Reason are correct, but Reason is not the correct explanation for the Assertion.
- **(B)** Assertion and Reason are correct and Reason is the correct explanation for the Assertion.
- **(C)** Assertion is incorrect, Reason is correct.
- **(D)** Assertion is correct, Reason is incorrect.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) Assertion is correct, Reason is incorrect.**

**Detailed Solution:**

Electron density between nuclei increased during formation of BMO in <math><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> .

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/102121.PNG" style="width: 500px; height: 106px;" />

BMO is <math><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">ψ</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">A</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">ψ</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">B</mtext></mrow></msub></math> (Linear combination of Atomtextc orbitals) provides constructive interference.
While by distructive interference anti BMO <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>ψ</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> <mo>−</mo><msub> <mtext>ψ</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> formed.

</details>

### Q65. JEE Main 2014 (11 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The correct order of bond dissociation energy among $\mathrm{N}_2, \mathrm{O}_2, \mathrm{O}_2^{-}$is shown in which of the following arrangements?

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\mathrm{N}_2>\mathrm{O}_2^{-}>\mathrm{O}_2$
- **(B)** $\mathrm{O}_2^{-}>\mathrm{O}_2>\mathrm{N}_2$
- **(C)** $\mathrm{N}_2>\mathrm{O}_2>\mathrm{O}_2^{-}$
- **(D)** $\mathrm{O}_2>\mathrm{O}_2^{-}>\mathrm{N}_2$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $\mathrm{N}_2>\mathrm{O}_2>\mathrm{O}_2^{-}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

The bond order of $\mathrm{N}_2, \mathrm{O}_2$, and $\mathrm{O}_2^{-}$are respectively 3,2 and $1.5$
Since higher bond order implies higher bond dissociation energy hence the correct order will be
$$
\mathrm{N}_2>\mathrm{O}_2>\mathrm{O}_2^{-}
$$

</details>

### Q66. JEE Main 2014 (09 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The number and type of bonds in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> ion in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CaC</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> are:

**Options:**

- **(A)** Two <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></math>-bonds and two <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></math>-bonds
- **(B)** One <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></math>-bond and two <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></math>-bonds
- **(C)** One <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></math>-bond and one <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></math>-bond
- **(D)** Two <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></math>-bonds and one <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></math>-bond

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) One <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></math>-bond and two <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></math>-bonds**

**Detailed Solution:**

The structure of calcium carbide is:

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>Ca</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>

Both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> atoms are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>sp</mi></math> hybridised. The carbide anion, having <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math> units of negative charge between two carbon atoms, has <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></math> bonds.

</details>
